 X_i=\begin{cases}
 Y_i-ha_i',&i\in D',\\
 ht/d-Y_i/d,&i\in F'.
 \end{cases}
 \tag{2.4}
\]

This is the parent's genuine inverse with omega=1_(F'). Indeed, with Z=t+da'-Y,

\[
 Z\ge0,\qquad
 \mathbb E[1_{F'}Z]=p_Ft+d\mathbb E[a'1_{F'}]-\mathbb E[Y1_{F'}]
 =d\mathbb E a'.
\]

Thus E X=0; its actual projection at threshold t is -a' on D' and X-t on F'; and X-h P_(K_a')X=Y atom by atom. Atoms in F' are also unsaturated at this newly added query. Atoms in D' satisfy X_i<=0: for old frozen atoms this is immediate, and for newly retired positive Y_i, (2.1) gives X_i<=-Y_i/d<0. Therefore the three invariants hold for the next reverse step.

There is no omitted cap exception: (2.1)--(2.4) hold for every d>0, both d<1 and d>=1. A reflected free atom can become positive or negative; it remains free and is repaired by (2.3) if needed. A frozen atom is never reflected again. No saturation event is erased.

After a finite word, all wall labels, weights and X_0 have been fixed. Forward execution uses this one final function. Later wall modifications have preserved every previously constructed physical iterate and function value; there is no changing objective during that forward run.

### 2.3 A concrete multivalue terminal reserve and exact radius accounting

For a word of length n with every step >1, take M>=n+1 free atoms. Give one frozen atom mass 1/2 and terminal value -q. Give the M free atoms masses 1/(2M) and terminal values q+eta_i, where

\[
 q>2,\quad \sum_i\eta_i=0,\quad |\eta_i|\le1/2.
\]

Initially use wall 1 everywhere. The terminal threshold is q-1. The frozen gradient is -1, and the free gradients are 1+eta_i. Thus all M reserve atoms are genuinely unsaturated at the terminal query. Retiring one atom per reverse step leaves at least one movable atom throughout. This gives a complete deterministic construction, with a genuinely multivalue terminal law if the eta_i are not all equal.

Writing overline(eta^2)=M^-1 sum eta_i^2, its preserved terminal value and variance are

\[
 2F_a(X_n)=2q-1+\tfrac12\overline{\eta^2},\qquad
 \|X_n\|^2=q^2+\tfrac12\overline{\eta^2}.
 \tag{2.5}
\]

At every reverse step, the exact original-metric cost of the new predecessor is

\[
 \|X\|^2=
 \mathbb E[1_{D'}(Y-ha')^2]
 +d^{-2}\mathbb E[1_{F'}(ht-Y)^2].
 \tag{2.6}
\]

In particular the term h^2 E[1_(D') (a')^2] is paid. Large walls on rare newly retired atoms are not free merely because their terminal gradients stay unchanged. Equations (2.1)--(2.6), applied to the complete given word, specify actual finite values and an exact radius; they are not an unproved radius bound.

If desired, choose q so that (2.5) equals B_(n_m). The construction then has terminal benchmark value exactly B_(n_m). The next result shows why its radius cannot meet the required benchmark estimate on H_m.

## 3. All-depth obstruction for the entire monotone-face class

The recursion above has active sets that only expand in forward time. This is a restriction on the actual physical trajectory, not a guess about its endpoint law. The following derivation covers every K_a trajectory with that property, including arbitrary finite weights, arbitrary nonnegative walls, arbitrary terminal laws and arbitrary numbers of atoms.

### 3.1 Orthogonal geometry of nested simplex faces

For a nonempty coordinate set A, let

\[
 p_A=P(A),\quad b_A=\frac{\mathbb E[a1_{A^c}]}{p_A},\qquad
 (e_A)_i=\begin{cases}b_A,&i\in A,\\-a_i,&i\notin A,\end{cases}
\]
\[
 L_A=\{v:\operatorname{supp}v\subset A,\ \mathbb E v=0\}.
\]

The minimum-norm point on the face with g=-a off A is e_A. Its affine hull is e_A+L_A, and e_A is orthogonal to L_A. If the projection of X lies on this face and is unsaturated on A, then

\[
 g=e_A+P_{L_A}X.
 \tag{3.1}
\]

For A contained in B, e_A-e_B lies in L_B and is orthogonal to L_A and e_B. Refining a nested chain one added atom at a time therefore gives mutually orthogonal nonzero increments

\[
 v=e_A-e_{A\cup\{i\}}.
\]

Each such v is a supporting normal of K_a on the smaller face: its largest coordinate is constant on A, and all other coordinates are smaller. Since g+a>=0 and E(g+a)=E a,

\[
 \langle g,v\rangle\le\|v\|^2\quad(g\in K_a),
 \tag{3.2}
\]

with equality on that smaller face. This uses the actual weighted Euclidean geometry; no non-isometric coordinate transform is inserted.

### 3.2 Simultaneous releases and their chronology

Suppose the actual active sets satisfy

\[
 A_0\subseteq A_1\subseteq\cdots\subseteq A_n.
 \tag{3.3}
\]

Boundary atoms may be assigned consistently to either side. At a step from A=A_(r-1) to B=A_r, define the old slack for each entering atom by

\[
 s_i=t_{r-1}-a_i-X_{r-1,i}\ge0.
\]

Refine this simultaneous release by adding the entering atoms in nondecreasing order of s_i. This refinement introduces no extra physical time or query; it only chooses an orthogonal basis.

For an intermediate set A'=A union J and the next entering atom i, direct averaging at the old query gives

\[
 \operatorname{mean}_{A'} X_{r-1}-X_{r-1,i}
 =b_{A'}+a_i+s_i-\frac{\mathbb E[1_Js]}{p_{A'}}.
 \tag{3.4}
\]

The last two terms have nonnegative difference by the chosen order. For the associated nonzero increment v, its normalized X coefficient is

\[
 \xi_{r-1}:=\frac{\langle X_{r-1},v\rangle}{\|v\|^2}
 =\frac{\operatorname{mean}_{A'}X_{r-1}-X_{r-1,i}}{b_{A'}+a_i}
 \ge1.
 \tag{3.5}
\]

At all earlier physical queries the actual face is contained in the smaller refined face, so the gradient coefficient on v is exactly 1. At and after time r it equals the X coefficient, by (3.1). At time r, (3.2) gives xi_r<=1. If S_<r denotes the sum of steps preceding the release step and that step is h=1+d, these facts give the exact chronology

\[
 \xi_{r-1}=\xi_0-S_{<r}\ge1,\qquad
 \xi_r=\xi_0-S_{<r}-h\le1,
\]
\[
 S_{<r}+1\le\xi_0\le S_{<r}+h+1.
 \tag{3.6}
\]

After the release, xi is multiplied by 1-h_t at every subsequent step. Thus the contribution of this entire mode to terminal twice-value, divided by its original squared radius contribution, is at most

\[
 \max\left\{
 \frac{d^2\prod_{\text{after }r}(h_t-1)^2}{(S_{<r}+1)^2},\quad
 \frac{\prod_{\text{after }r}(h_t-1)^2}{(S_{<r}+h+1)^2}
 \right\}.
 \tag{3.7}
\]

The maximum occurs at an endpoint of (3.6), because |1-(S_<r+h)/xi_0| has its maximum there. Importantly, an atom that enters late cannot bring an arbitrary uncharged quadratic deviation: (3.4)--(3.6) tie that deviation to its original radius.

For completeness, zero walls do not create a missing uncharged direction. A zero increment means b_(A')=a_i=0. All walls outside A' are then zero. Use the contrast direction positive and constant on A', negative on i, and zero elsewhere. Its old gradient coefficient is zero. By (3.4) and the same slack ordering its old X coefficient is nonnegative. Feasibility of every g in K_a makes its gradient coefficient nonpositive. After i is active the gradient coefficient equals the X coefficient; that X coefficient did not change in the release step because its old gradient coefficient was zero. It must therefore vanish. Its original coefficient also vanishes. Such directions can be discarded, rather than divided by a zero norm.

### 3.3 The remaining modes and exact original-radius comparison

Directions in L_(A_0) are quadratic from time 0 and have terminal-to-initial squared ratio product_t(h_t-1)^2. The last face has an unreleased normal e=e_(A_n). If e is nonzero and xi_t=<X_t,e>/||e||^2, its gradient coefficient is 1 throughout, so xi_n=xi_0-S_n. Projection optimality against 0 gives xi_n>=1. Its terminal twice-value is

\[
 (2\xi_n-1)\|e\|^2,
\]

and its original radius contribution is xi_0^2||e||^2. Their ratio satisfies

\[
 \frac{2(\xi_0-S_n)-1}{\xi_0^2}\le\frac1{1+2S_n}.
 \tag{3.8}
\]

Any residual original component orthogonal to L_(A_n) and e is orthogonal to every gradient in the chain; it stays constant and contributes only nonnegative additional radius. At the terminal point the envelope formula is exactly

\[
 2F_a(X_n)=\|P_{L_{A_n}}X_n\|^2
            +2\langle X_n,e\rangle-\|e\|^2.
 \tag{3.9}
\]

Equations (3.7)--(3.9) therefore bound the actual full ratio by the largest mode ratio. There is no replacement of the original radius by a radius at a release time.

### 3.4 Evaluate the bound on the complete H_m

Every suffix product of the positive excesses h-1 in H_j is at most 1. This follows by induction: a suffix either lies in the right child, or contains that child and the bridge, whose product is d_(j-1)P_(j-1)<1/c, times a suffix of the left child. Empty suffixes have product 1.

Let E_j be the largest of the two **unsquared** expressions in (3.7), over release steps anywhere in the complete H_j, with the real prefix sums in that word. For a release in its left child, the two quantities are multiplied by d_(j-1)P_(j-1)<1/c. At the bridge and throughout the right child, the denominators are at least 1+S_(j-1), while the required suffix products are at most 1. Therefore

\[
 E_j\le\max\{E_{j-1}/c,\ (1+S_{j-1})^{-1}\},\qquad E_0=0,
\]
\[
 E_j\le\frac1{u c^{j-1}}\quad(j\ge1).
 \tag{3.10}
\]

Consequently every released-mode payoff on H_m is at most

\[
 E_m^2\le(c^2/u^2)c^{-2m},
\]

and every initially quadratic mode is at most P_m^2<c^(-2m). For m>=1536 these are below 1/D_m: it suffices that c^m>2c^2/u^2, whose right side is <50, whereas c^m>2^m. The affine bound (3.8) is 1/D_m. Thus

\[
 \boxed{\quad
 \frac{2F_a(X_n)}{\|X_0\|^2}\le\frac1{D_m}
 \quad\text{for every nested-active-set }K_a\text{ trajectory on }H_m.
 \quad}
 \tag{3.11}
\]

The standard constant-gradient Huber trajectory attains 1/D_m, so this upper bound within the stated trajectory class **with selected minimizer 0** is exact. In particular the concrete reserve recursion in Section 2 obeys

\[
 \|X_0\|^2\ge D_m\,[2F_a(X_n)].
\]

At terminal benchmark value B=B_(n_m), it requires original variance at least B D_m; its benchmark-normalized payoff is at most B/D_m<47/50. This rules out the whole monotone-reserve invariant, even with arbitrarily many terminal values and different walls. Adding old auxiliary-query preservation requirements only restricts this class further.

The conclusion does **not** cover a trajectory that leaves and later reenters an active face. It does not prove W_(n_m)(H_m)<=1/D_m for general functions or for all K_a trajectories.

## 4. Approach 2: pay for a real change between opposite saturated faces

The preceding obstruction forces actual recycling, so this approach abandons nested active sets. It uses a different fixed body when multiple reserves are combined: an orthogonal product of interval projection bodies. Each component executes the whole physical word and has a fully charged original radius.

### 4.1 An exact paid-reflection component for an arbitrary word

Take any predetermined nonnegative word and a selected step h_i=1+d>1. Write

\[
 P=\sum_{t<i}h_t,\qquad R=\sum_{t>i}h_t.
\]

For A>0 define the asymmetric Huber function

\[
 f_A(x)=\max_{g\in[-A,1]}\{xg-g^2/2\}.
\]

It is convex, 1-smooth, and minimized at the original 0. Its gradient is clip(x,[-A,1]). It is also a two-atom K_a instance in the weighted mean-zero metric: take X=(x,-x), equal weights, and a=(A,1).

Choose any q>=1, and fix

\[
 A=(q+R)/d,\qquad x_0=-A(1+P).
 \tag{4.1}
\]

Throughout the prefix the gradient is -A and the trajectory ends at x_i=-A. The chosen step gives

\[
 x_{i+1}=dA=q+R.
\]

Throughout the suffix the gradient is 1, and the actual terminal value is x_n=q. The two opposite gradient faces are attained; clipping is strict at the initial query when P>0, and at the post-bridge query when q+R>1. In particular both strict events occur at an internal bridge of H_m with nonempty children. Every step, including zeros elsewhere in the word, is accounted for. The wall A is chosen once before forward execution.

The exact original radius and terminal twice-value are

\[
 x_0^2=A^2(1+P)^2,\qquad 2f_A(x_n)=2q-1,
\]

so the paid payoff is

\[
 \frac{d^2(2q-1)}{(1+P)^2(q+R)^2}.
\]

Its maximum over q>=1 is attained at q=R+1. Thus a real full-word witness has

\[
 \boxed{\quad
 L_i(h)=\frac{(h_i-1)^2}{(1+P)^2(1+2R)},\quad
 A=\frac{1+2R}{h_i-1},\quad x_0=-A(1+P),\quad x_n=1+R.
 \quad}
 \tag{4.2}
\]

The factor A^2(1+P)^2 is exactly the cost of preparing the reflected reserve through the entire prefix. It cannot be replaced by the radius at x_i=-A. This is an actual face-change construction, not an increase of a formerly saturated wall while pretending its old query survives.

### 4.2 A recursive bank with several terminal values

One can build a finite reserve bank on any binary parse of the physical word: at each node whose bridge exceeds 1, add a component (4.2), using the bridge's **global** P and R in the complete word; recurse into the left and right node lists. Nodes with bridge at most 1 add no reflection component. Also retain a baseline Huber component with payoff 1/(1+2 sum h).

For arbitrary positive spatial scales lambda_i, set

\[
 F(x)=\sum_i\lambda_i^2 f_{A_i}(x_i/\lambda_i),
 \qquad (x_0)_i=\lambda_i[-A_i(1+P_i)].
 \tag{4.3}
\]

The gradient is 1-Lipschitz in the ordinary product Euclidean norm. The selected minimizer is 0. Every component runs the same entire physical word without a reset. Its terminal coordinate is lambda_i(1+R_i), so the bank can have many terminal values and many actual face changes at different times. Its original squared radius and terminal objective are exactly the sums of the component quantities.

Consequently its total normalized payoff is the radius-weighted average of its component payoffs. The bank cannot exceed their largest value. This statement is about the explicit product body (4.3); it is not a claim that a coupled simplex K_a decomposes into independent reservoirs.

### 4.3 Quantitative obstruction on every node of the complete H_m

Consider a step at level j in H_m. Its excess is d=a_h c^j. Put s=S_j. The actual global prefix and suffix sums satisfy P>=s and R>=s, because the occurrence has a full H_j child on each side. Also

\[
 \frac d{1+s}\le c-2=:r<3/7.
 \tag{4.4}
\]

Let T=P+R=S_m-(1+d). At fixed T, the denominator in (4.2) is

\[
 (1+P)^2[1+2(T-P)].
\]

Its logarithm is concave on s<=P<=T-s, so its minimum occurs at an endpoint. The smaller endpoint is P=s: the function (1+x)^2/(1+2x) is increasing for x>=0. Therefore

\[
 D_m L_i(H_m)
 \le\frac{d^2}{(1+s)^2}\,
 \frac{D_m}{D_m-2s-2(1+d)}.
\]

The last fraction decreases as D_m increases, and the actual condition R>=s gives D_m>=4s+2d+3. Hence

\[
 D_m L_i(H_m)
 \le\left(\frac d{1+s}\right)^2
       \frac{4s+2d+3}{2s+1}
 \le r^2(3+2r)
 <\frac{243}{343}<1.
 \tag{4.5}
\]

This covers every occurrence and every inherited level with the same enclosing parameters. Even after q and its wall A are optimized, every paid-reflection component in this bank is uniformly worse than the standard Huber component. Any finite positive combination has payoff at most 1/D_m, and hence benchmark-normalized payoff below 47/50.

At the root alone, (4.2) gives the particularly transparent complete-H_m witness

\[
 \frac{d_{m-1}^2}{(1+S_{m-1})^2D_{m-1}}.
\]

It has the correct order c^(-m), but a fixed loss. Formula (4.5), rather than this order statement, is the decisive reason this recursive independent-bank repair cannot close the target.

## 5. What is closed and what is still missing

Approach 1 supplies a genuine reserve recursion that can be executed at every reverse step on H_m, with all eventual wall labels fixed before forward execution and with exact original-variance accounting. Its tempting invariant is quantitatively insufficient: the entire nested-active-set class at the selected original minimum 0 has exact best payoff 1/D_m. Persistent physical unsaturation is therefore not by itself the missing target-level mechanism. Old auxiliary queries cannot repair this loss.

Approach 2 changes the physical mechanism: it crosses opposite saturated faces and pays the preparation radius. Its full-word witnesses and multivalue product bank are realizable, and (4.5) excludes the entire specified bank on H_m. The failure is the cost of the globally prepared reflected coordinates, rather than an unsupported claim that their wall labels were free.

Neither derivation excludes a **coupled** multivalue K_a trajectory with repeated exits and reentries of active faces. Such a trajectory would have to exploit correlations between reserve coordinates that are absent from (4.3), and it would not obey the orthogonal one-release decomposition used in (3.11). No construction or benchmark-scale original-radius upper bound for that coupled mechanism has been obtained in this invocation.

In particular there is no proved uniform all-word lower bound (1-o(1))/B_n, no full-class asymptotic upper certificate, and no complete candidate for formal audit. The unchanged low-mass Huber argument still covers sum h<=(B_n-1)/2. The exact all-integer-split DP has not been replaced by the diagnostic hierarchy.

## 6. Resource and closeout record

- Assigned native model invocation: 1, gpt-6-astra / ultra.
- Substantive approaches: 2, completed to the scoped outcomes above.
- Mathematical programs, numerical arrays, solvers: 0.
- Formal submissions and verifier calls: 0.
- Additional agents/models, renewals, worker or service starts: 0.
- Access: designated public sources, operating instructions, writing skill, and a bounded memory-registry check; no private worker memory.
- Writes: this PROOF_ATTEMPT.md and HANDOFF.md in the assigned packet only.
- Budget, Goal, truth stores, OPERATOR, CURRENT_RUN, all other packets, publication, finalization and revocation: untouched.
- Exact token usage is not exposed to this child. The parent owns the authoritative backend/budget receipt; no token count is invented.

Stop after this bounded allocation. Original target ACTIVE / OPEN. All new mathematics UNVERIFIED.

# Handoff: full weighted-prefix finite PEP

**UNVERIFIED DISCOVERY. Goal ACTIVE, original target OPEN.** Allocation `6b000743076cec7f` is CLOSED. This is a fresh grant, separate from the completed analytical allocation `bfc190fee04e9d8d`.

## What ran

One native `gpt-6-astra` / `ultra` invocation, one batch, exactly five precharged whole-process slots of 20 seconds each. One CVXPY/CLARABEL solve per slot; no retry/fallback. Both calibrations passed, then `P_3,P_4,P_5` ran using the same enclosing `m_env=1536` parameters. All statuses were `optimal` / `Solved`, all export sets complete, all warning lists empty.

The full primal Gram PEP maximized

`V_pref=2 f_p-(1-a_last^2)||g_p||^2`

under original radius `||x0||^2<=1`, the full PSD Gram on `(x0,g0,...,g_p)`, and **every** ordered smooth-convex interpolation row on `star,0,...,p`. No rank, source-weight, common-column, tree, diagonal-residual, or support condition was imposed. Every incoming row at the final prefix query was kept.

For production, `eps=1/(4*1536)`, `beta=1-eps`, `c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+eps)`, `a=beta/c`, `H_0=()`, `H_(r+1)=(H_r,1+a*c^r,H_r)`. Each prefix deletes only the actual final step `1+a`; `D_j=1+2 sum H_j`. Full words, prefixes and matrices are saved.

## Exact calibration premises

The empty-prefix calibration has `a_last=1/2` and exact optimum `4/7`, with certificate

`(4/7)||x0||^2-2f0+(3/4)||g0||^2+2Q_(0,star)=(7/4)||g0-(4/7)x0||^2`.

A global Huber function with cap `4/7`, at `x0=1`, attains it. The second calibration has prefix `(1)`, `a_last=0`, exact optimum `1/4`, and multipliers `(0,star):1/2`, `(1,star):3/2`, `(0,1):1/2`, `(1,0):1`. Its remainder is

`||x0-g0-3g1||^2/4+||g0-g1||^2/4`.

A cap-`1/4` Huber function attains it with `g0=g1=1/4`, `x1=3/4`. `DERIVATION.md` proves global convexity, 1-smoothness, attained minimum, all signed rows and exact attainment before freeze. Charged `Fraction` checks returned zero exact differences; raw floating maximum was `2.7755575615628914e-17`, preserved under the `1e-7` gate.

Saved calibration objective errors: `9.500078501645248e-11`, `1.129529803023388e-11`. Both full gates PASS; exit zero alone was not used as the gate.

## Numerical outcome

| Case | `D_j V_pref` | `D_j q_dual` | Primal minus `17/16` |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| `P_3` | 1.079536836925768 | 1.0795368390342717 | +0.017036836925768073 |
| `P_4` | 1.0307918140421866 | 1.0307918147264237 | -0.03170818595781344 |
| `P_5` | 1.0124176155927562 | 1.0124176174000286 | -0.050082384407243774 |

Unscaled primal values: `0.03977600183919927`, `0.015398842868376537`, `0.006209991968555536`. Raw dual-minus-primal gaps: `7.768872350988332e-11`, `1.0221715834868306e-11`, `1.108549016470839e-11`.

The production raw Gram minimum eigenvalues are `-2.2697398248745334e-11`, `-3.39535029749277e-12`, `-3.639481083660732e-12`. Original signed-row maxima are positive: `3.712325585825127e-11`, `8.951519213373782e-12`, `1.465013483789579e-11`. Every original dual polynomial also has a tiny negative eigenvalue; full stationarity and all raw spectra are retained.

Accordingly there is no exact primal witness or exact dual upper certificate. Coordinate extraction was explicitly skipped for every production case; no clipping or repair occurred. The numerical `P_3` failure is of the sufficient **weighted-prefix** estimate at that finite child depth. Smoothness only says `2 f_after <= V_pref`; the failure does not supply a counterexample to the actual full-`H_3` objective. Nor does a depth-three child diagnostic disprove a statement required only at sufficiently large enclosing depth. `P_4/P_5` are finite positive evidence, not an all-depth theorem.

## Durable evidence

- `REPORT.md`: all saved comparisons, unrepaired defects, scope, normalization and resource receipt.
- `DERIVATION.md`, `CASES.json`, `OUTPUT_SCHEMA.md`, `child.py`: frozen exact model, calibration premises, fixed cases, exports and mathematical source.
- `FREEZE.json`, `PREPARATION_RECEIPT.json`, `NATIVE_REGISTRATION.json`, budget snapshots and `input_context/`: 31 execution-container-verified frozen entries and separate host/container provenance.
- `raw/<case>/source_frame.json` and `input_arrays.npz`: full literal words and all original coefficient matrices.
- `raw/<case>/arrays.npz`, `transcript.json`, `result.json`, `solver_data.json`, `canonical_data.json`, `program_data.npz`, `exact_fixtures.json`: complete raw results and diagnostics.
- Per-case precharges, receipts and logs; top-level `registration.jsonl`, `budget.json`, `CLOSED.json`: actual execution/accounting.

Current TASK SHA:

`9014fa480ce83890df24bc670dd6209cdf5811229fee869f4171e4f94b4847a7`.

Allocated v1 SHA: `404b50980d6ae1ccd3bab84aa417013104e1f8a994c43eb6962749b12ecb1c49`.

Freeze SHA: `6bc451ca70f8cd489f9240ca7afa8e110076a95abcbc665ececb478a4423bfcd`.

## Closed resources and next boundary

Native registration exactly once; 5 jobs / 100 accounted seconds / `32.778605112998775` actual whole-child seconds. Zero jobs or seconds remain, zero unused slots, formal attempts zero. `CLOSED.json` reports `all_five_jobs_accounted`, all frozen hashes unchanged, `STOP=true`. Initial sandbox Docker access failed before registration; the authorized escalated preparation and the single fresh batch succeeded. No mathematical probe or test run preceded freeze.

After closure only saved-text transcription, reports, byte hashes and accounting are allowed; no arrays were reloaded/reanalyzed. No extra model/agent, consultation, install, worker/service action, private-memory read, truth-store edit, publication, finalization, revocation, shutdown or Goal mutation occurred. Exact reconstruction or new depths require a fresh allocation. **STOP; no renewal.**

# Handoff: weighted-prefix analytical grant bfc190fee04e9d8d

**JOINT UNVERIFIED. Original target OPEN.** This fresh native invocation is complete. The parent owns budget closure. No renewal is authorized by this handoff.

## Read first

- `REPORT.md`, all sections, particularly the complete original-row derivation in Sections 2-3 and the uniform bound in Section 6.
- Original `PROBLEM.md` and current `OPERATOR.md`.
- Accepted facts `1252b8823bb5a290` and `931b9653ce0f7201`, at their stated scopes.

TASK hash checked: `83b6f143538f091bfa95725950425ffe8321c0ece8a59925974d81d7255501c3`.

## Exact object

For one enclosing `m>=1536`, keep

`eps=1/(4m), beta=1-eps, c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+eps), a=beta/c, b=1+a`,

`H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), n=2^m-1, l=n-1, S=sum H_m, D=1+2S`.

The actual prefix removes only the final physical step `b`. The sought estimate is

`2 f_l-(1-a^2)||g_l||^2 <= q||x_0||^2`, `q=17/(16D)`.

Smoothness on the actual last update `x_n=x_l-bg_l` gives

`2 f_n <=2 f_l-(1-a^2)||g_l||^2`.

Thus the target would imply the required full-class upper bound, but this invocation supplies no proof of that target.

## Full matrix retained

Let `L_ii=1`, `L_ij=h_j` for `j<i`, and zero above its diagonal. Put

`v=L^T e_l=(h_0,...,h_(l-1),1)`, `T=sum v=S-a`.

For all original queried-prefix rows, use `mu_i=lambda_(i,star)`, `nu_i=lambda_(star,i)`, `N_ij=-lambda_ij` off the diagonal, `N_ii=mu_i+sum_(j!=i)lambda_ij`, and `B=sym(NL)`.

The exact coefficient conditions are

`N e=mu`, `N^T e=2e_l+nu`, `mu,nu>=0`, `N_ij<=0` for `i!=j`.

The complete polynomial is PSD exactly when

`K=B-a^2 e_l e_l^T-mu mu^T/(4q) >=0`.

No diagonal, rank, primitive-energy, fixed-common-column, or Huber-null restriction is placed on `K`.

## Completed two-law outcome

Law 1 was `mu=kappa v/T`, `kappa>=2`, permitting any nonnegative `nu` of mass `kappa-2`. It fails the source necessity `mu_0<=4q`.

The second and final law was

`d=b/2`, `w=v-d(e_0-e_l)`, `mu=kappa w/T`, `kappa>=2`.

At `kappa=2`, this repairs the source screen: `0<mu_0=b/T<4q`. Every query-to-query row, incoming minimizer profile of the necessary mass, and full non-diagonal residual remain free.

At the physical root set `k=2^(m-1)-1`, `H=sum_(j<k)h_j`, `R=h_k`, `z=1_[0,k)`, and `mu_L=sum_(j<k)mu_j`. The exact nonnegative original-row cut is

`C=H mu_L-z^T B z+a(mu_k-B_kk)`

`=sum_(j<k)(H-t_j)nu_j`

` +sum_(i>k,j<k)(H-t_j)lambda_ij`

` +sum_(j<k)(H-t_j-a)lambda_(k,j)`

` +a(R-1)sum_(j>k)lambda_(k,j) >=0`.

Full PSD gives `z^T B z>=mu_L^2/(4q)` and `B_kk>=mu_k^2/(4q)`. With `J_0=H-d`, law 2 has `mu_L=kappa J_0/T`, `mu_k=kappa R/T`. The report proves manually and uniformly

`C < -31 H/204000 < -H/7000 <0`

for every `m>=1536` and `kappa>=2`. Therefore this whole source-balanced law is excluded, including every completing skew/remote array, every incoming minimizer profile, and every full PSD residual.

## What is still missing

A different, genuinely free profile `mu`, together with `nu` and the original query array, must be constructed if this selected certificate route is to prove the target. The necessary conditions

`mu_0<=4q`,

`mu_L^2+a mu_k^2 <=4q(H mu_L+a mu_k)`,

`sum mu=2+sum nu`

are not sufficient and do not supply a recursive all-depth law. No such law is obtained here. Changing only the residual matrix cannot repair either frozen profile.

This is not a counterexample to the prefix inequality or to the actual final-iterate bound. Do not promote the obstruction to all source profiles or to the unrestricted target. The full old `8bcf300ff83e2288` report and the dense `A/D` report were read; their fixed columns and diagonal-residual assumptions were not inherited. The choice of the last prefix sample is not presented as novel or as success of the interrupted old worker.

## Resource receipt

Mathematical programs, numerical or symbolic replays, arrays, solvers, formal submissions, consultations, additional agents/models, installations, lifecycle/truth-store operations, publication, finalization, revocation, and shutdown: all zero. # R4 handoff

Status: original exact GD minimax target OPEN. No complete candidate for formal review; all new mathematics is an unverified analytical draft.

Read PROOF_ATTEMPT.md for the self-contained statements and proofs. This was one native analytical invocation, with two substantive proof lines, mathematical PROGRAM0, numerical/array/solver0, formal0, and no additional agent/model. No worker, service, truth-store, Goal, publication or finalization action was taken.

## What was actually constructed

1. A fixed finite-dimensional function pasting two complete projection-type GD histories using one auxiliary coordinate. Equations (4)--(10) specify all points, gradients, values and cross-history rows; (11) pays the original initial radius. The full function is the quadratic-regularized support function of one finite gradient hull. It is chosen once before the run.
2. A fixed convex 1-smooth function realizing a terminal transverse-gradient perturbation of a strictly quadratic prefix. Its necessary and sufficient finite interpolation budget is (18), and (19) is an explicit conjugate construction, including an attained minimum. This is only a realized prefix, not a completed adverse trajectory for the original problem.
3. Within the same second proof line, an actual shared-history pasting with no auxiliary initial radius: a rotated quadratic prefix satisfying (22), followed by the metric cap (23)--(24). Equations (25)--(28) check every old and cross row, give the original-radius payoff, and give explicit scalar parameter conditions. This is a full-word hard function under those conditions, not a universal all-word construction.

## Decisive checks

The first construction cannot itself provide the sharp target uniformly. On two nonzero quadratic Silver children, its generated errors have an all-depth ceiling

\[
\limsup_m B_{2^{m+1}-1} E_m\le2(\sqrt2-1)<1.
\]

This uses the exact benchmark recurrence and the analytic coarse estimate \(B_n\le4(n+1)^\rho\), not numerical recurrence evaluation. The polar-cone check is included: for a nonzero negative clock gradient the minimizer is unique, and at zero negative clock gradient 0 remains the closest minimizer. The ceiling is therefore not an artifact of selecting a distant minimizer.

Scope is crucial: this ceiling concerns the displayed pasted witnesses. It is not a bound on \(W_n\), and it does not exclude a larger proof strategy that selects a global Huber or quadratic witness on these same words. Do not record it as an obstruction to the original conjecture or to every conceivable static gluing.

The second line directly attacked the auxiliary-radius loss by sharing coordinates. An already fixed unit-curvature prefix can force the next bridge and a unit step to land at the minimum, so no unrestricted extension theorem can use an arbitrary such child history. A transverse endpoint perturbation is realizable only within the terminal-scale slack (18); an initial-radius estimate cannot substitute for that slack. Changing the whole shared history with two quadratic modes avoids that endpoint-only restriction. The actual cap in (22)--(28) does complete a compatible whole trajectory without adding radius. Its available prefix test requires a unique spectral sign-changing earlier step larger than the selected bridge, and its moment interval excludes a unit bridge. Arbitrary-word availability, recursive compatibility and the exact benchmark payoff remain unproved.

## What remains missing

There is no construction selecting compatible shared histories for every nonnegative word, no proven arbitrary-word split, and no uniform loss sum producing \(1-o(1)\) with the exact \(C_n/B_n\) recurrence. The optional negative direction did not produce a full-function upper certificate or asymptotic separation. The gap has the strength of the actual target; it is not an unproved technical lemma.

No formal review or automatic renewal is requested by this handoff. The one-call/two-line allocation is finished. Parent owns shared registration and administrative closeout.

# Direct closure attempt R4: static pasting and its unresolved radius tradeoff

Date: 2026-10-08. Status: UNVERIFIED analytical draft; original target OPEN.

This invocation used two substantive lines: an actual static pasting with one auxiliary coordinate, then a shared-coordinate replacement. It produced no complete affirmative or negative proof. The first construction below is an admissible hard function, with its original radius and every cross-history row paid explicitly. Its quantified failure concerns that construction, not the original conjecture or a family augmented by other witnesses. The second line did not produce a uniform all-word pasting.

No mathematical program, numerical evaluation, array, solver, additional model, or formal submission was used. Text and primary-source reads and manual derivation were used. No truth store was changed.

## 1. Target and normalization

For a nonnegative predetermined word \(h=(h_0,\ldots,h_{n-1})\), GD is

\[
x_{i+1}=x_i-h_i\nabla f(x_i)
\]

after setting \(L=1\). The supremum defining \(W_n(h)\) is over all finite dimensions, convex differentiable functions with 1-Lipschitz gradient and an attained minimum, every selected minimizer, and every nonzero initial radius:

\[
W_n(h)=\sup\frac{2(f(x_n)-f_\star)}{\|x_0-x_\star\|^2}.
\]

Let \(C_0=B_0=1\), and, for every integer split \(k+l=n-1\), let

\[
\Omega(x,y)=\frac{x+y+\sqrt{x^2+6xy+y^2}}2,
\quad
G(x,y)=2x+\frac y2+\frac{\sqrt{y^2+8xy}}2,
\]

\[
C_n=\max_{k+l=n-1}\Omega(C_k,C_l),
\qquad B_n=\max_{k+l=n-1}G(C_k,B_l).
\]

The required affirmative result is

\[
\forall\epsilon>0\ \exists N_\epsilon\ \forall n\ge N_\epsilon\
\forall h\in[0,\infty)^n:
\quad W_n(h)\ge\frac{1-\epsilon}{B_n},
\]

with \(N_\epsilon\) independent of \(h\). A negative resolution would instead require a rigorous full-function upper certificate with a fixed asymptotic improvement over \(1/B_n\). Neither was obtained.

The source examined was Zhang--Jiang, [v2](https://arxiv.org/html/2410.12395v2), especially Sections 2.2, 3.1 and 3.2, Lemma 15 and the retained square in the dominant composition. These prove sufficient compositions; their reversal was not assumed. Proposition 24, also checked in that source, gives \(C_n\le(n+1)^\rho\), where \(\rho=\log_2(1+\sqrt2)>1\).

## 2. Calibration checked independently

For a word split at \(\alpha>1\), write \(x,y\) for the masses before and after the bridge, \(C=1+x\), \(b=\alpha-1\). The single fixed function with derivative

\[
f'(z)=\operatorname{clip}(z,-r,1),\qquad f(0)=0,
\]

started at \(C\), reaches 1 before the bridge and \(-b\) after it. With \(r=b/(2y+1)\), its whole suffix remains on the negative affine ray and

\[
\frac{2f(z_n)}{C^2}=\frac{b^2}{C^2(2y+1)}.
\]

Balancing this with the whole-word Huber witness \(1/[2C+2b+(2y+1)]\) yields

\[
(b+C)\{2b^2+(2y+1)b-C(2y+1)\}=0.
\]

The positive root is the stated \(\psi\) bridge. With \(r=b/(y+1)\), the terminal is \(-r\), the rows \(Q_{i,\star}=f_i-f_\star-\langle g_i,x_i-x_\star\rangle+\|g_i\|^2/2\) are zero, and the primitive retained energy is

\[
\frac12\{C^2-(x+\alpha+y+1)^2r^2\}.
\]

Its zero is \(b(C+(y+1)+b)=C(y+1)\), the stated \(\varphi\) bridge. These are calibration, not a converse or a recursive construction.

## 3. Line 1: an actual static pasting of two complete histories

### 3.1 A concrete finite-dimensional function

For a finite set of gradients whose convex hull (K) contains 0, define

\[
F_K(X)=\max_{g\in K}\{\langle X,g\rangle-\tfrac12\|g\|^2\}.
\tag{1}
\]

The maximizing gradient is (P_KX). Its uniqueness gives differentiability, and the projection inequalities give

\[
\|P_KX-P_KY\|^2\le\langle X-Y,P_KX-P_KY\rangle.
\]

Thus (1) is convex and 1-smooth. Also (F_K\ge0=F_K(0)). A proposed jet ((X_i,g_i)) is realized, with value (\langle X_i,g_i\rangle-\|g_i\|^2/2), precisely when

\[
\langle X_i-g_i,g-g_i\rangle\le0\quad(g\in K).
\tag{2}
\]

It suffices to check (2) at all generating gradients, including 0. This gives one fixed function after the whole word has been selected; it is not an adaptive reselection.

### 3.2 Inputs and the pasted trajectory

Let the prefix word have length \(k\), mass \(\sigma\), and partial masses \(s_i\), \(0\le i\le k\). Let its complete projection-type history be \((u_i,a_i)\), including its terminal gradient. Let the suffix word have length \(l\), mass \(\tau\), partial masses \(t_j\), and complete history \((v_j,b_j)\), \(0\le j\le l\). Both histories satisfy GD and (2), with their own 0 included. Their values are denoted \(F_A(u_i)\), \(F_B(v_j)\).

Fix any bridge \(\alpha>1\), and put \(d=\alpha-1\). Choose

\[
r\ge0,\quad\theta\ge0,\quad
\theta+(\tau+1)r\le d,\quad p>0,
\qquad T=(\sigma+1+\theta)p.
\tag{3}
\]

In the orthogonal space of dimension (d_A+d_B+1), prescribe the prefix jets

\[
X_i=(u_i,v_0,T-s_ip),\qquad A_i=(a_i,0,p),
\tag{4}
\]

and suffix jets

\[
Y_j=(c,v_j,(\theta-d+t_jr)p),\qquad
D_j=(0,b_j,-rp),\qquad c=u_k-\alpha a_k.
\tag{5}
\]

Each block has exactly its prescribed GD recursion, and

\[
X_k-\alpha A_k=Y_0.
\]

The coordinates called inactive here really are frozen along the displayed trajectory: their gradients are 0 in the relevant block. This is enforced by the global interpolation below, not assumed from a direct sum.

### 3.3 Every old row, cross row, and minimizer row

Take (K=\operatorname{conv}(\{0\}\cup\{A_i\}\cup\{D_j\})). Within each block, its projection rows are exactly the original child rows; the added gradient coordinate is constant. The rows against 0 hold because the original rows hold and the clock products are nonnegative by (3).

For a prefix normal against a suffix gradient, (2) is exactly

\[
-\langle u_i-a_i,a_i\rangle+\langle v_0,b_j\rangle
-(\sigma-s_i+\theta)(1+r)p^2\le0.
\tag{6}
\]

For a suffix normal against a prefix gradient it is exactly

\[
\langle c,a_i\rangle-\langle v_j-b_j,b_j\rangle
-[d-\theta-(t_j+1)r](1+r)p^2\le0.
\tag{7}
\]

The original minimizer rows imply that both subtracted child inner products are nonnegative. Consequently define

\[
M_1=\max(0,\max_j\langle v_0,b_j\rangle),\qquad
M_2=\max(0,\max_i\langle c,a_i\rangle),\qquad M=\max(M_1,M_2).
\]

The explicit choice

\[
\theta=d/4,\qquad r=\frac d{2(\tau+1)},\qquad
p^2\ge\frac{4M}{d(1+r)},\quad p>0
\tag{8}
\]

satisfies all rows: each clock margin in (6)--(7) is at least (d(1+r)p^2/4). Thus (1), with this finite (K), realizes the full pasted history.

The child function values are not silently reset. The exact global values are

\[
F_K(X_i)=F_A(u_i)+(\sigma-s_i+\theta+\tfrac12)p^2,
\tag{9}
\]

\[
F_K(Y_j)=F_B(v_j)+[r(d-\theta)-(t_j+\tfrac12)r^2]p^2.
\tag{10}
\]

Thus the old child contributions and all their internal interpolation rows are retained, with an explicitly specified lift of the global values. The displayed global data are fixed before any step and remain unchanged afterwards. This is not a zero-cost pasting of the original unlifted jets.

### 3.4 The actual original-radius lower witness

Using the valid selected minimizer 0, the original radius and terminal value give

\[
W_{k+l+1}(h_A,\alpha,h_B)\ge
\frac{2F_B(v_l)+2[r(d-\theta)-(\tau+\tfrac12)r^2]p^2}
{\|u_0\|^2+\|v_0\|^2+(\sigma+1+\theta)^2p^2}.
\tag{11}
\]

Zero steps inside either child cause no difficulty in the formulas. Children may be empty. The construction accepts arbitrarily large finite bridges (\alpha>1). It does not prescribe an arbitrary-word decomposition, and (11) has not been shown to match the exact (B_n) recursion.

### 3.5 An all-depth audit of this particular pasting

The auxiliary radius is a real loss, not just an overly distant selected minimizer, on the following family.

Write (\lambda=1+\sqrt2), (q=\sqrt2-1=1/\lambda), and (c_m=\lambda^m). Define the Silver words

\[
s_0=(),\qquad s_{m+1}=(s_m,1+qc_m,s_m).
\]

Direct induction gives length (2^m-1), mass (c_m-1), and, for (m\ge1),

\[
\prod_{h\in s_m}(1-h)=-1/c_m.
\tag{12}
\]

Take both child histories to be nonzero one-dimensional unit-curvature quadratic trajectories on (s_m), use the bridge (1+qc_m), and paste by (3)--(5). Their terminal quadratic ratios are (1/c_m^2). For any parameters satisfying (3), the clock-only terminal ratio is

\[
\frac{2r(d-\theta)-(2\tau+1)r^2}{(c_m+\theta)^2}
\le\frac{(d-\theta)^2}{(2\tau+1)(c_m+\theta)^2}
\le\frac{q^2}{2c_m-1}.
\tag{13}
\]

The full ratio in (11) is a weighted average of this ratio, the suffix quadratic ratio (1/c_m^2), and a zero contribution from the frozen prefix coordinate. Therefore the actual normalized error (E_m) produced by every such feasible pasted instance satisfies

\[
E_m\le\max\{c_m^{-2},q^2/(2c_m-1)\}.
\tag{14}
\]

For \(r>0\), these pasted functions have a unique minimizer in their three-dimensional space. Indeed \(\arg\min F_K=K^\circ\), since \(\nabla F_K=P_K\). Each nonzero Silver quadratic child contains positive and negative scalar gradients. The prefix inequalities for \(z\in K^\circ\) force \(z_{\rm clock}\le0\); the suffix inequalities force \(z_{\rm clock}\ge0\). Then both scalar child components must vanish. Thus \(K^\circ=\{0\}\), and changing the selected minimizer cannot improve (14). If \(r=0\), the polar cone instead forces \(z_B=0\), \(z_{\rm clock}\le0\). The initial prefix gradient gives \(u_0z_A+pz_{\rm clock}\le0\), so \(\langle X_0,z\rangle\le(T-p)z_{\rm clock}\le0\). Hence 0 is still the closest minimizer and the denominator conclusion remains valid.

For comparison with the exact benchmark, (G(x,y)\le4x+y), as (\sqrt{y^2+8xy}\le y+4x). Combining this with the source bound (C_n\le(n+1)^\rho), induction over the exact integer-split recurrence gives

\[
B_n\le4(n+1)^\rho.
\tag{15}
\]

In that induction use (a^\rho+b^\rho\le(a+b)^\rho), with (a=k+1,b=l+1). At (n_m=2^{m+1}-1), ((n_m+1)^\rho=\lambda c_m); (14)--(15) imply

\[
\limsup_{m\to\infty} B_{n_m}E_m
\le 2\lambda q^2=2(\sqrt2-1)<1.
\tag{16}
\]

This is an all-depth failure of the displayed pasted witnesses. It is not an upper bound on (W_{n_m}(s_{m+1})), and it does not exclude a proof that chooses a global Huber or quadratic witness instead. In particular, those alternative witnesses must not be discarded when interpreting (16).

The responsible cross row can also be seen without the final ratio. Choose the first child's terminal (a>0), so its start is (-c_ma). Its post-bridge frozen coordinate is (-da). Because the second child has zero quadratic residual, (7), applied to the old start and the suffix terminal, requires

\[
[d-\theta-c_mr](1+r)p^2\ge dc_ma^2.
\]

Since (r\le d/c_m=q), necessarily (p^2\ge c_ma^2/(1+q)), and (T^2\ge c_m^3a^2/(1+q)). The old prefix radius was only (c_m^2a^2). This explains why making the auxiliary coordinate larger eventually replaces the child information by a single-cut clock witness.

## 4. Line 2: shared evolving coordinates

### 4.1 Exact pasting without any extra initial coordinate

Suppose a projection-type prefix ends at (u_k=a_k=a\ne0). A bridge (\alpha=1+d) sends it to (-da). Embed a suffix history by an isometry and a common scaling so that its start is exactly (-da), and take the convex hull of the two sets of gradients and 0 in their shared space. This produces an actual global function (1) if and only if its cross projection rows hold. If they hold, there is no added original radius, and the terminal ratio is the suffix ratio multiplied by (d^2\|a\|^2/\|u_0\|^2).

This was attacked at the actual histories rather than declared as a gluing lemma. A unit-curvature quadratic prefix exposes the obstruction. If its queried jets include opposite points on a line and satisfy \(g_i=x_i\), \(f_i=\|x_i\|^2/2\), then every convex 1-smooth extension agreeing with those jets is forced to be the same quadratic on the segment between them. To see this, \(H(x)=\|x\|^2/2-f(x)\) is convex for convex 1-smooth \(f\); at both endpoints \(H=0\) and \(\nabla H=0\). Convexity forces \(H=0\), hence \(\nabla f(x)=x\), on the entire segment.

For (m\ge2), a quadratic prefix on (s_m), started at (-c_ma), has queried points (-c_ma) and (qc_ma>0), and terminal (a). The next bridge (1+qc_m) lands at (-qc_ma), inside that already forced quadratic segment. A following unit step reaches the minimizer 0 exactly and all later steps stay there. Adding dimensions with zero initial components and keeping these old jets cannot change that fact. Thus a recursive rule cannot extend this already chosen near-extremal prefix history through an arbitrary suffix at no cost.

This is a failure of a universal history-preserving child-extension claim. The original adversary may choose a different prefix history after seeing the entire word, so it does not disprove the target.

### 4.2 A realizable transverse perturbation, with its exact budget

To test whether a small curvature loss repairs that obstruction, let (0<\kappa<1) and let (u_0,\ldots,u_k\) be the GD history of the quadratic (\kappa\|u\|^2/2) on the prefix. Embed it with a new coordinate initially 0. Retain all old jets for (i<k), and replace only the terminal gradient by

\[
x_k=(u_k,0),\qquad
g_k=(\kappa u_k,\eta),\qquad
f_k=\kappa\|u_k\|^2/2.
\tag{17}
\]

The prefix GD positions are unchanged, because this terminal gradient has not yet been used. For every earlier jet, its two directed interpolation slacks against (17) are both

\[
\frac{\kappa(1-\kappa)}2\|u_k-u_i\|^2-\frac{\eta^2}2.
\]

Both directed minimizer slacks are

\[
\frac{\kappa(1-\kappa)}2\|u_k\|^2-\frac{\eta^2}2.
\]

Thus all these rows hold exactly when

\[
\eta^2\le\kappa(1-\kappa)
\min\bigl(\{\|u_k\|^2\}\cup
\{\|u_k-u_i\|^2:0\le i<k\}\bigr).
\tag{18}
\]

A repeated query \(u_i=u_k\) forces \(\eta=0\), which matters for unrestricted words including zero steps.

For completeness, these consistent finite jets have a concrete static realization. Include the minimizer jet ((0,0,0)), and, over all prescribed jets, set

\[
H(g)=\frac12\|g\|^2+
\max_i\left\{\phi_i+\langle x_i-g_i,g-g_i\rangle\right\},
\qquad
\phi_i=\langle g_i,x_i\rangle-f_i-\tfrac12\|g_i\|^2,
\qquad F=H^*.
\tag{19}
\]

The interpolation inequalities say that the (i)-th affine term is active at (g_i); hence (x_i\in\partial H(g_i)). The quadratic term makes (H) 1-strongly convex, so (F) is finite, differentiable and 1-smooth with the specified jets. The included 0 jet and its inequalities give (H(0)=0), (0\in\partial H(0)); thus (F(0)=0) is an attained minimum. Formula (19) realizes (17) before the bridge, without a function change afterwards.

The scale in (18) is the terminal distance, not the original radius. If a proposed prefix has \(\|u_k\|\le K R/C\), its bridge obeys \(\alpha\le A C\), and \(R=\|u_0\|\), then

\[
\alpha|\eta|\le AK R\sqrt{\kappa(1-\kappa)}.
\tag{20}
\]

For (1-\kappa=O(C^{-2})), this is only (O(R/C)) transverse displacement. In particular, the absolute statement “gradient of size (1/\alpha) costs curvature (1/\alpha^2)” cannot be applied to a normalized near-extremal prefix whose terminal radius is itself of order (R/C). No conclusion about the full future objective is inferred from (20) alone.

### 4.3 Changing the whole shared history

A two-mode quadratic provides a literal shared-history replacement, not an endpoint-only injection. For orthogonal eigenvectors

\[
v_1=(\cos\theta,\sin\theta),\qquad
v_2=(\sin\theta,-\cos\theta),
\]

curvatures \(\lambda_1,\lambda_2\in(0,1]\), initial point \(e_1\), and

\[
P_t(z)=\prod_{i<t}(1-h_i z),
\]

the actual quadratic trajectory has transverse coordinate

\[
y_t=\sin\theta\cos\theta[P_t(\lambda_1)-P_t(\lambda_2)].
\]

If (P_k(\lambda_1)=P_k(\lambda_2)=P\ne0), then (y_k=0), but its transverse gradient is

\[
\sin\theta\cos\theta P(\lambda_1-\lambda_2).
\]

All past values, gradients and the initial radius now belong to a single actual quadratic; (18) does not apply because the whole history changed. Keeping this quadratic for the whole word, however, gives exactly

\[
2f(x_n)=\cos^2\theta\,\lambda_1P_n(\lambda_1)^2
+\sin^2\theta\,\lambda_2P_n(\lambda_2)^2,
\]

a convex combination of scalar quadratic errors. Rotation alone therefore supplies no stronger witness than the scalar quadratic family. A nonquadratic suffix is still necessary for this proposed repair to do additional work.

One direct attempted static extension is

\[
F(X)=\sup_{g\in K}\{\langle X,g\rangle-\tfrac12g^T A^{-1}g\},
\tag{21}
\]

where \(A\) has the two curvatures and \(K\) contains every old quadratic gradient and 0. All old quadratic jets are retained automatically, and \(0\prec A\preceq I\) makes (21) 1-smooth. This gives real freedom to cap future gradients without paying a new initial coordinate. It also exposes the exact sticking point: whenever \(AX\) lies in the old gradient hull, its unconstrained maximizer is still available, so (21) is forced to take the original quadratic gradient at \(X\). The future cap cannot simply be placed across that hull.

### 4.4 A shared-history cap that does paste exactly

There is a concrete special case of (21) where the cap can be placed and all cross rows close with no auxiliary initial radius.

Let the actual two-dimensional quadratic prefix have Hessian \(A\), \(0\prec A\preceq I\), initial point \(e_1\), and points \(x_i\) with

\[
e_2^Tx_i\ge0\quad(0\le i\le k),\qquad x_k=P e_1.
\tag{22}
\]

All these are statements about the actual prefix trajectory. Its old gradients are \(g_i=Ax_i\), its values are \(x_i^TAx_i/2\), and its original radius is 1.

Suppose the bridge \(\alpha\), suffix mass \(\tau\), and numbers \(r>0,s\ge0\) satisfy

\[
P(e_1-\alpha A e_1)
=-sA^{-1}e_2-rA^{-2}e_2,\qquad s\ge\tau r.
\tag{23}
\]

Set \(b=-rA^{-1}e_2\), and use the single fixed function (21) with

\[
K=\operatorname{conv}(\{0,b\}\cup\{Ax_i:0\le i\le k\}).
\tag{24}
\]

Each old gradient is its unconstrained quadratic maximizer in (21), so every old point, gradient and function value is unchanged. For a suffix partial mass \(t_j\), prescribe

\[
X_j=-sA^{-1}e_2-rA^{-2}e_2+t_jrA^{-1}e_2.
\]

This is exactly the GD recursion if the suffix gradient is \(b\). Its metric projection normal is

\[
q_j=X_j-A^{-1}b=-(s-t_jr)A^{-1}e_2.
\]

For every old gradient,

\[
\langle q_j,Ax_i-b\rangle
=-(s-t_jr)e_2^Tx_i
-r(s-t_jr)\|A^{-1}e_2\|^2\le0.
\tag{25}
\]

The row against 0 is also nonpositive, and the row against \(b\) is zero. Thus \(b\) is exactly the maximizing gradient throughout the suffix, including its terminal point. All old-to-new rows hold because the old metric projection normals are 0; (25) checks the reverse direction. All data come from the one function (21),(24).

It follows, with the same original start and selected minimizer 0, that this admissible full-word instance has

\[
2F(X_l)
=r^2 e_2^TA^{-3}e_2
+2r(s-\tau r)e_2^TA^{-2}e_2.
\tag{26}
\]

No child was restarted and no initial coordinate or radius was added. The steps after the bridge may be any nonnegative word with the prescribed mass \(\tau\); individual zero or large steps do not invalidate the monotonic partial-mass check.

The parameter constraints in (23) can be checked without an unproved geometric lemma. Write

\[
m_j=e_1^TA^je_1,\quad D=\det A,\quad a_{12}=e_1^TAe_2\ne0.
\]

Solving the two linear equations in (23), or using Cayley--Hamilton, gives

\[
s=\frac{P}{a_{12}}(\alpha m_3-m_2),
\qquad
r=\frac{PD}{a_{12}}(m_1-\alpha m_2).
\tag{27}
\]

When \(P/a_{12}>0\), a sufficient and, apart from endpoint degeneracies, necessary interval for \(r>0,s\ge\tau r\) is

\[
\frac{m_2+\tau Dm_1}{m_3+\tau Dm_2}
\le\alpha<\frac{m_1}{m_2}.
\tag{28}
\]

The interval is nonempty for a genuinely mixed pair of distinct positive eigenvalues because \(m_2^2<m_1m_3\). That inequality follows by Cauchy--Schwarz applied to the eigenvalue moments.

Condition (22) also has a concrete sufficient prefix test. In the rotated two-mode setup of Section 4.3, let \(0<\lambda_1<\lambda_2\le1\), \(0<\theta<\pi/2\), assume

\[
P_k(\lambda_1)=P_k(\lambda_2)=P<0,
\]

and suppose exactly one prefix step exceeds \(1/\lambda_2\). Equality and the negative product imply that this unique step exceeds \(1/\lambda_1\), and that no other factor vanishes. Before that step both products are positive and their ratio is at least 1. After it both products are negative; their ratio is nondecreasing and ends at 1, hence is at most 1. Therefore \(P_i(\lambda_1)-P_i(\lambda_2)\ge0\) for every earlier query, giving (22). Also \(a_{12}<0\), so \(P/a_{12}>0\) as required in (28).

This sufficient test has a substantial restriction: (28) forces \(\alpha< m_1/m_2\le1/\lambda_1\), whereas the unique earlier sign-changing step exceeds \(1/\lambda_1\). The selected bridge is therefore smaller than that earlier step. It cannot be applied automatically to the maximum step of an arbitrary word. A unit bridge is also excluded in the genuinely mixed case because \(m_2/m_3>1\) when the positive eigenvalues are at most 1 and are not both 1.

Equations (22)--(28) are an actual compatible-prefix/static-suffix construction. What is missing is not its interpolation: it is a proof that every word needing a stronger witness admits such a compatible prefix or a recursively compatible replacement, and that the payoff (26), with all previous history retained, yields the exact \(B_n\) bound. No such selection or uniform loss sum was obtained. This special pasting therefore does not close the original target and is not asserted to do so.

## 5. Closure audit and stopping status

The auxiliary-coordinate construction passes static realizability, finite dimension, attained minimum, GD chronology, original-radius accounting, and explicit cross-history interpolation. The all-depth ceiling (16) applies only to its stated witness family and includes a minimizer-set check. For any prescribed \(L>0\), replacing a constructed \(F\) by \(LF\) gives an \(L\)-smooth function, leaves normalized GD unchanged, and leaves \(2(f_n-f_\star)/(L\|x_0-x_\star\|^2)\) equal to the displayed ratio.

The endpoint-only replacement has an exact realizable prefix (17)--(19), but no all-word suffix construction. Changing the whole shared history by an actual two-mode quadratic leads to the exact static cap (22)--(28), preserving every old value and the original radius under its explicit conditions. Those conditions have not been proved available for arbitrary words or closed under repeated pasting.

The missing work is the mathematical content of the original problem: a choice of globally compatible histories for every word, or a different sharp universal lower argument, together with a schedule-independent (1-o(1)) loss against the exact integer-split profile. It is not a technical interpolation check and was not assumed as a lemma. No full-function asymptotic upper certificate beating (1/B_n) was produced either.

This invocation stops at its one-call/two-line allocation. The original target remains OPEN; none of these drafts is submitted or accepted as a project fact.

## DOC-000126 — archive_core_20261010/gd-exact-profile-20261010/materials/historical_reports/direct_closure_update_20261008_r4.md

证据标签：source_document_no_new_verification。以下内容原样收录，不因汇编而获得新的证明地位。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000126 -->
# 直接闭合尝试：当前判断

已按新的要求调整研究重心：以张–蒋原文保留的联合不等式和精确拼接常数为出发点，直接构造适用于原问题的困难函数。新的电网络参数化没有启动；先前两条矩阵路线已按额度结束，没有继续派生同类变体。

原目标仍是对每个充分大的长度、每个预定非负步长序列证明

\[
\mathcal W_n(h)\ge(1-o(1))/B_n,
\]

其中误差必须与具体步长无关，\(U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}=1/B_n\)，归一化为 \(\mathcal W=2\mathcal R\)。如果答案是否定的，则需要全函数类的严格渐近分离。当前两者均未得到。

这次直接调用实际构造了同一个静态凸光滑函数中的轨迹拼接，逐项处理了原有查询、跨段插值约束和初始半径。结果支持以下判断：

- 增加固定时钟坐标的办法可以实现拼接，但会付出不消失的半径代价。它不能单独承担精确常数的统一闭合。这个结论只针对该构造；不能据此排除同时使用 Huber 等其他困难实例的完整策略。
- 仅在已有近二次前缀的末端增加横向梯度，受到末端尺度的严格插值余量限制，不能按原始半径估算扰动成本。
- 同时改变共享历史，可构造一个不增加初始半径的真实拼接，并得到明确的函数与参数公式。不过，当前可实现条件要求桥接步小于前缀中的某个大步，不能直接沿最大步切分做归纳。它还没有覆盖任意步长串，也没有可反复使用的兼容性或统一损失估计。

因此目前没有完整候选可送审。后续工作的实质是选择能在整个已知步长串中兼容的历史，而非假设任意固定子历史都能无损延长。张–蒋的充分拼接定理也不能直接作为任意步长的必要结构。

完整静态构造和全部条件见 `research/zhang_jiang_direct_closure_20261008_r4/PROOF_ATTEMPT.md`，续接范围见同目录 `HANDOFF.md`。本次直接调用及两条此前已分配的 R4 调用均使用 Astra / ultra，数学程序和正式验证均为零；新增数学仍是未验证草稿。Goal 保持 ACTIVE，原问题保持 OPEN。

<!-- END_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000126 -->

## DOC-000127 — archive_core_20261010/gd-exact-profile-20261010/materials/historical_reports/research_update_20261008_resume2.md

来源：D:\Danus\runtime\handoffs\gd-exact-profile-20261010-core.zip!/gd-exact-profile-20261010/materials/historical_reports/research_update_20261008_resume2.md

证据标签：source_document_no_new_verification。以下内容原样收录，不因汇编而获得新的证明地位。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000127 -->
# 恢复研究后的更新：全图证书与递推缺口

2026 年 10 月 8 日。Goal 已恢复 active；原始渐近 minimax 问题仍未解决。本文整理本轮已经完成的工作，没有把数值解或受限证书障碍作为原问题的答案。新解析结论均为未正式核查接收的草稿。

最有用的新线索是：允许自由吸收权重、保留完整非对角二次型时，七步和十五步基块的全图模型都出现了正裕量数值解。纯树模型为负。随后尝试了两种保留完整子矩阵的稠密拼接法则，均找到明确障碍，因此尚不能从这些有限结果递推到任意深度。

## 原目标与本轮接口

对预定非负步长 h，W_n(h) 是全部有限维光滑凸函数上的末点误差上确界，归一化为2(f_n-f_star)/(L||x0-xstar||^2)。目标是判断 W_n^star/U_n^ZJ 是否趋于1，或者给出严格的渐近分离。

当前候选步长族始终使用同一个外层参数 m>=1536：

$$
\varepsilon=\frac1{4m},\quad\beta=1-\varepsilon,\quad
c=(1+\sqrt2)(1+\varepsilon),\quad a=\beta/c,
$$
$$
H_0=(),\qquad H_{j+1}=(H_j,1+ac^j,H_j).
$$

记 S_j 为 H_j 的步长和、D_m=1+2S_m。已接收的基准比较说明：如果对所有充分大的 m 证明完整函数类上的

$$
W_{2^m-1}(H_m)\le\frac{17}{16D_m},
$$

就能把 Zhang–Jiang 基准比值压到799/800以下。这个上界仍然缺失。

投影模型取

$$
F_D(x)=\max_{g\in D}\{\langle x,g\rangle-\tfrac12\|g\|^2\},
\qquad \nabla F_D=P_D,
$$

其中 D 为含零的紧凸集。定义

$$
U_d(x)=\max_{g\in D}\{2\langle x,g\rangle-d\|g\|^2\}.
$$

精确传播 U_1(T_hx)<=U_(1+2sumh)(x) 是一个更强的充分目标。本轮先研究这一传播式的原始投影法向证书；后续也可以直接研究放宽17/16后的传播式。

## 全图与树图的有限数值对照

程序保留全部梯度坐标、辅助投影坐标和非对角项，吸收权重可以自由变化。gamma 是满足“原始商矩阵减去 gamma 倍指定半正定度量仍半正定”的最大矩阵裕量；它不是 W，也不是收敛率。

两项校准分别具有精确裕量1/3和-1/4，均通过预先固定的检查。四个正式案例都返回 optimal/Solved：

| 物理步长块 | 树形连接 gamma | 全部原始点对连接 gamma |
|---|---:|---:|
| H3，七步 | -0.447725545 | 0.017349318 |
| H4，十五步 | -0.750250476 | 0.005948800 |

全图原始商矩阵的最小特征值分别约0.0166250和0.00577637。优化裕量之后的余项仍有约10^-11量级的小负特征值，线性约束也有浮点误差，均原样保留。因此目前得到的是有限数值候选，而非精确证书或全深度结论。[完整数值报告](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/projection_absorption_exactfixture_20261008_r2/REPORT.md)

此前一次校准曾因1/M的浮点展开无法达到严格零差异而停止。新批次使用有理数检查固定恒等式，同时保留浮点展开误差；优化器、算例和数值精度门槛没有改变。旧失败记录及退役额度没有被重写或复用。

## 新解析草稿排除了什么

第一类是从两列单调系数生成的稠密插值乘子，并要求剩余项只包含非负对角平方。变量步长恒等式本身正确，而且17/16目标可以转成一个明确的终端惩罚阈值。但是，完整前缀恒等式和中央切分条件共同迫使“中央步长减一／左块容量”小于sqrt(2)-1，而实际候选序列违反它。因此整个指定系数族都失败，任意非负对角余量不能修复。[完整推导](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/dense_history_search_20261008_r2/REPORT.md)

第二类是单辅助投影、指定二进制树上的完整原始法向证书。主代理草稿给出更一般的必要条件：在完整 H_m 的中央大步长处，向过去的总流量必须大于

$$
\frac{ac^{m-1}}{16}-2.
$$

而该树的每条向下边至多承载2，中央节点只有 m-1 个子节点。因此即使吸收权重全部放开，这个树结构也不能覆盖所需大深度。该草稿尚未独立审查；它不排除完整图、不构造违反目标上界的函数。[流量必要条件与树障碍](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/main_tree_absorption_obstruction_20261008_r2.md)

## 从全图数值解到递推公式还缺什么

为了保留子块的完整信息，提取了两个联合特征：

$$
A=\sum_{i<t}h_i(g_i-g_t),\qquad B=z-g_t,
$$

其中 z 是子块吸收权重给出的梯度凸组合。保存的数值矩阵可以给出(A/S,B)的联合二次型控制，混合项没有被删除，完整子矩阵的剩余部分也被保留。

但两条明确的稠密拼接规则仍然失败：

- 以子块吸收概率的乘积连接两个子块，会要求 h_i/pi_i 对时间坐标呈仿射关系。七步候选所在的一个宽邻域不满足该条件。
- 让目标侧法向矩精确匹配两个联合特征的非负组合，会产生一个严格负的原始连接权重；该负权重不能剪掉。

这些是两种指定规则的障碍，不是所有联合能量方法或所有稠密拼接的排除。完整的父块恒等式已经保存，但其余项的任意深度半正定估计仍未完成。[联合储备与两条拼接的完整报告](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/projection_joint_reserve_search_20261008_r2/REPORT.md)

## 高维下界构造的现状

Gray 路线新增了两种势函数的统一失败推导；它们不排除任意初始化。随后真正优化了两个有限字典的全部初始化，D_j乘程序目标值约为0.6502、0.6597；第二例状态为optimal_inaccurate，两例保留正约束残差且未独立计算真实最近距离。目前未得到所需强度的下界见证，也不能据此外推到完整2^m维构造。[全初始化数值报告](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/gray_full_perspective_positivecal_20261008_r2/REPORT.md)

## 下一步应改变的结构

值得保留的是完整原始约束、非树连接和非对角余项。已排除的对角化系数族、纯树结构及两条具体拼接规则不宜重复调参。

一个尚未分配的新方向是直接瞄准

$$
U_1(T_hx)\le\frac{17}{16}U_{1+2\sum h}(x).
$$

初步系数推导显示，这会允许总量1/8的零点来源约束权重。相应二次型在所有梯度相等时不再必须为零，所以旧的时钟接触条件、辅助点吸收性质以及固定末端梯度为零的化简，都必须重新检查。该变化尚未产生证明，仅作为下一轮明确的研究接口。[未审查的放宽接口](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/main_relaxed_u_interface_lead_20261008_r2.md)

本轮没有新增正式核查提交。已完成的解析调用和数值预算均已关闭，原始 Goal 保持active。精确证据位置、资源边界和恢复顺序见[当前接续记录](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/RESUMED_HANDOFF_20261008_R2.md)。

<!-- END_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000127 -->

## DOC-000128 — archive_core_20261010/gd-exact-profile-20261010/materials/historical_reports/research_update_20261008_resume3.md

来源：D:\Danus\runtime\handoffs\gd-exact-profile-20261010-core.zip!/gd-exact-profile-20261010/materials/historical_reports/research_update_20261008_resume3.md

原始字节数：6295；原始 SHA-256：3812afc8bc60981432dc3769f4c581d6ebadc3e784ac303b1dbe59ef03cd8787

证据标签：source_document_no_new_verification。以下内容原样收录，不因汇编而获得新的证明地位。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000128 -->
# 2026 年 10 月 8 日续研结果：完整前缀目标与回流结构

原问题仍未解决，Goal 保持进行中。本轮的有效进展是：获得了带解析校准的完整有限步性能估计，并把若干失败的系数尝试归并为一个范围明确的整类障碍。没有新的正式验证提交，也没有把数值结果或解析草稿提升为已接受事实。

## 原目标和当前使用的充分条件

原问题保持

\[
\mathcal W_n(h)=\sup\frac{2(f(x_n)-f_\star)}{L\|x_0-x_\star\|^2},
\qquad
\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{\inf_{h\ge0}\mathcal W_n(h)}{U_n^{\rm ZJ}}=1\ ?
\]

上确界包含所有有限维凸光滑函数、原有选定最小点及初始点；步长预先确定，允许零步长和任意大的有限步长。归一化始终为 \(\mathcal W=2\mathcal R\)。肯定答案需要对所有充分大长度、所有非负步长序列一致成立；否定答案需要真实全函数类的渐近分离。

当前否定路线考察同一外层深度 \(m\) 下的递归步长

\[
\epsilon=\frac1{4m},\quad \beta=1-\epsilon,\quad
c=(1+\sqrt2)(1+\epsilon),\quad a=\frac\beta c,
\qquad H_{j+1}=(H_j,1+ac^j,H_j),\ H_0=().
\]

每个子块使用同一个 \(m\)，不能在子块重新设置参数。令 \(D_m=1+2\sum H_m\)。已有范围明确的已接受结果给出 \(B_{2^m-1}/D_m<47/50\)，其中 \(U_n^{\rm ZJ}=1/B_n\)。如果能证明原函数类上

\[
\mathcal W_{2^m-1}(H_m)\le\frac{17}{16D_m},
\]

就会得到比值的下极限不超过 \(799/800\)，从而否定原极限等于一。这里缺失的仍然是这个全深度上界。

## 完整前缀性能估计

删去 \(H_j\) 最后一步 \(b=1+a\)，得到前缀 \(P_j\)。若前缀末点为 \(x_p\)，最后一步的光滑性保证

\[
2(f_{p+1}-f_\star)\le
V_{\rm pref}:=2(f_p-f_\star)-(1-a^2)\|g_p\|^2.
\]

因此，控制此前缀目标是一个充分方向。有限步程序保留完整 Gram 矩阵、原始初始半径、所有有序凸光滑插值约束，以及最小点两个方向的约束；没有限制矩阵秩、权重形式或边的支撑。

先用解析最优值分别为 \(4/7\) 和 \(1/4\) 的两个问题校准，两项均通过。随后固定外层参数 \(m=1536\)，计算三个较小子块：

| 前缀 | 实际前缀更新次数 | 数值 \(D_j V_{\rm pref}\) | 相对 \(17/16=1.0625\) |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| \(P_3\) | 6 | 1.0795368369 | 高于阈值 |
| \(P_4\) | 14 | 1.0307918140 | 低于阈值 |
| \(P_5\) | 30 | 1.0124176156 | 低于阈值 |

三次求解均返回最优状态，但原始矩阵仍保留约 \(10^{-11}\) 至 \(10^{-12}\) 量级的负特征值和正约束残差。因此这里没有精确可实现的下界实例，也没有精确可行的上界证书。尤其 \(P_3\) 的越界不是实际 \(H_3\) 末步目标值的反例，更不能否定仅要求充分大深度的命题。

一个可直接手推的 Huber 实例，对任何非负前缀都给出

\[
\sup V_{\rm pref}\ge\frac1{D_j-b^2}.
\]

提取结果显示三次数值最优值均与该下界接近。它提示了可能的精确值，但没有证明所有深度的等式。前缀目标若要低于所需阈值，至少必须有 \(D_j\ge17b^2\)。

## 解析搜索得到的边界

本轮另有几项解析结果，均是尚未正式验证的草稿。

- 多尺度投影点：得到一个明确的非负平方和传递恒等式，也补出两个实际投影点之间的径向平方项。但两个拼接规则都被内部相反的局部梯度扰动排除；这些形式扰动不是合法 GD 轨迹。仅在初始点和最右侧子块设置辅助点，不足以修复这两个规则。
- 分层源权重：有限证书显示内部左块和通向末端的分支具有不同权重形状。据此定义的一套解析源权重通过了此前的两个必要条件，但对称循环和按原步长比例落点的有向循环仍然失败。
- 边界回流：线性时钟权重因多层累积而超过首点上限；平方时钟权重通过了完整首点检查，但根部一个非零混合项与零对角项冲突，矩阵仍不定。

更强的结果是把最后这些失败归并起来。固定这套分层源权重 \(\mu\)，令末端查询 \(\ell\) 直接向各查询发送 \(\mu_i\) 的原有权重。如果保留这些边，只额外增加任意非负、流量守恒的查询循环，则完整 Schur 矩阵 \(K\) 必须满足

\[
z^T Kz+aK_{rr}<-\frac H{150}<0.
\]

这里 \(r\) 是整个递归块的中间大步查询，\(H\) 是其左块步长和，\(z\) 是左块查询的指示向量。这与正半定性矛盾。推导已被另一个解析调用独立检查，但尚未提交正式验证。

这个结论覆盖任意循环支撑、落点分布和强度，仍不覆盖所有对偶证书。它依赖“保留原末端边作为逐项下界”。下一步不能继续只在这个类别中增加循环。

主代理进一步写出的待核查推论是：若保持同一源权重而寻找一般证书，末端到左块的权重至少需要真正减少总量 \(1/150\)。减少是相对于旧构造而言；最终每条原始乘子仍必须非负，流量方程也必须重新满足。一个可具体尝试的后续方向，是先把部分末端回流送往较晚的左块查询，再在块内分配。尚无这类重分配的完整正半定构造。

## 计算与证据状态

本轮 R3 共六次有界原生研究调用，全部使用 Astra / ultra。数学程序只有五个校准/生产求解任务和一次既有结果提取，共预分配 120 秒，实际数学子进程约 34.05 秒；正式验证调用为零。其余为解析推导和共享结果整理。

截至 12:55 UTC，共享事实库仍为 18 项范围明确的已接受事实，18 条正式验证记录和一条历史工作者自检备注。本轮没有新增正式结果。原问题文件的哈希不变，没有重启旧 Danus 工作者，也没有发布或最终定稿。

完整推导与续接入口：

- `research/RESUMED_HANDOFF_20261008_R3.md`
- `research/weighted_prefix_full_pep_discovery_20261008_r3/REPORT.md`
- `research/prefix_source_profile_search_20261008_r3/REPORT.md`
- `research/prefix_boundary_transport_search_20261008_r3/REPORT.md`
- `research/main_fixed_terminal_fan_obstruction_20261008_r3.md`

原目标保持 OPEN，Goal 保持 ACTIVE。这份文档记录本轮结果，不是原问题的肯定证明或否定证明。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000129 -->
# 梯度下降精确常数问题阶段性总结

整理日期：2026 年 10 月 3 日。研究已按你的要求暂停，原始目标保持未解决。

目前最可靠的收获是：把原问题归约到有限几何模型，建立了完整终端证书接口，并核查接收了一个七步基块的精确最坏值。还没有得到原始渐近 minimax 等式，也没有得到足以否定它的全函数类上界。近期大量工作排除了具体证书形式、刻画了若干低维函数族；这些结果有助于选择下一步，但不能据此说已经接近完成主目标。

本文直接整理已经保存的材料，没有启动独立报告写作模型、重跑算例或进行新一轮证明核查。“已接收”指 Danus 核查记录已接收该明确陈述，不表示已经完成 Lean 等证明助手的形式化。尚未接收的推导、数值结果和存在错误的草稿分别标明。

**一、原始问题和目前选择的突破方向**

对任意预先给定的非负步长序列 $h=(h_0,\ldots,h_{n-1})$，考虑

$$
x_{t+1}=x_t-\frac{h_t}{L}\nabla f(x_t),\qquad
\mathcal W_n(h)=\sup\frac{2(f(x_n)-f_\star)}{L\|x_0-x_\star\|^2}.
$$

上确界覆盖所有有限维、具有最小点的 convex $L$-smooth 函数，以及所有合法起点和所选最小点。记

$$
\mathcal W_n^\star=\inf_{h\ge0}\mathcal W_n(h),\qquad
U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}=\frac1{B_n}.
$$

这里 $B_n=1+2S_\bullet(n)$ 是 Zhang–Jiang 的原始 dominant profile。我们要判断

$$
\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{\mathcal W_n^\star}{U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}}=1.
$$

肯定方向要求对所有充分大的整数 $n$ 和所有非负 $h$，统一证明 $\mathcal W_n(h)\ge(1-o(1))/B_n$。否定方向只需构造无界长度子序列及相应步长，并对**全部原始函数类**证明一个固定比例的改进。只匹配 Silver exponent、只处理少数长度或一个 adversary family，都不足以完成任务。全文保持 $\mathcal W=2\mathcal R$ 的归一化。

基准可等价写为 $C_0=B_0=1$，以及

$$
\begin{aligned}
\Omega(x,y)&=\frac{x+y+\sqrt{x^2+6xy+y^2}}2,\\
G(x,y)&=2x+\frac y2+\frac{\sqrt{y^2+8xy}}2,\\
C_n&=\max_{0\le k<n}\Omega(C_k,C_{n-k-1}),\\
B_n&=\max_{0\le k<n}G(C_k,B_{n-k-1}).
\end{aligned}
$$

这些最大值保留所有整数切分，不能用仅有平衡切分的序列替代。

**二、已经核查接收的主要数学成果**

| 成果 | 已建立的内容 | 对主目标的作用及边界 |
|---|---|---|
| Projection simplex 表示 | 每个固定有限步长序列的完整最坏值，可在下面定义的 origin-vertex simplex 函数类中取相同上确界 | 将任意光滑凸函数搜索转成有限几何问题；没有给出所需速率 |
| Affine-height Moreau envelope 表示 | 另一类至多 $n+2$ 个顶点的多面体模型，也保留完整有限序列上确界 | 提供了不同的具体构造接口；其梯度与前一模型不同 |
| Unqueried-terminal reduction | 完整性能估计可删去终点的 incoming rows，并选取相应紧的全空间证书 | 保留全部前缀约束；不允许随意删除远距离前缀行 |
| 精确基准比较 | $C_n\le(n+1)^\rho$、$B_n\le\frac{12}{5}(n+1)^\rho$，其中 $\rho=\log_2(1+\sqrt2)$；并得到下文候选步长族的比较桥梁 | 反例方向的基准算术已经落实；全函数类上界仍缺 |
| 实际七步基块 | 下文 $H_3^{(m)}$ 对全部原始函数类满足精确 Huber 值，并具有 all-body、all-$d$ 的投影不等式 | 是可靠基块；尚未推广到完整 $H_m^{(m)}$ |

Projection simplex 模型是

$$
F_D(x)=\max_{g\in D}\left\{\langle x,g\rangle-\tfrac12\|g\|^2\right\},
\qquad D=\operatorname{conv}\{0,w_1,\ldots,w_k\},
$$

其中 $w_1,\ldots,w_k$ 线性无关，$1\le k\le p+1$，$p$ 是序列中的正步数。它满足

$$
\nabla F_D=P_D,\qquad
\operatorname{argmin}F_D=C_D
=\{z:\langle z,g\rangle\le0\ \forall g\in D\}.
$$

对每个固定序列，限制到这个模型后的上确界等于原来的 $\mathcal W_n(h)$。证明使用严格光滑化、增加一个高度坐标保存有限轨迹，再作小扰动和维数压缩得到 simplex；所选最小点的距离在构造中被保留。结论是**上确界相等**，没有声称每条原始轨迹都由某个有限参数 simplex 精确表示，也没有给出对所有步长统一的条件数或逼近速度。完整最小点集合是极锥，不能一律把分母换成 $\|x_0\|^2$。[已接收的表示与七步结果](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/fact_graph/facts/1e0bf3f41457d8e4.md)

另一表示采用

$$
\Phi_{K,\ell}(X)=\min_{Y\in K}\left\{\langle\ell,Y\rangle+\tfrac12\|X-Y\|^2\right\},
\qquad \nabla\Phi_{K,\ell}(X)=X-P_K(X-\ell).
$$

其中 $K$ 是包含零的紧多面体，至多有 $n+2$ 个顶点，且 $\langle\ell,Y\rangle>0$ 对所有非零 $Y\in K$ 成立；取 $X_0\perp\ell$。该模型唯一最小点为零，并保持完整有限序列上确界。它与 $\nabla F_D=P_D$ 的模型不能混用。[已接收的 affine-height 表示](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/fact_graph/facts/d8b59c7d92425121.md)

终端化简则把上界问题写成完整原始插值行

$$
Q_{ij}=f_i-f_j+\langle g_i,x_j-x_i\rangle+\tfrac12\|g_i-g_j\|^2\le0
$$

的非负组合与 PSD 二次型。可以选取没有 incoming-terminal multipliers 的紧证书；该选择没有删除 queried prefix 内的约束。终点平方可精确剥离，剩下的是原始前缀空间上的完整 PSD 条件。这是后续系数搜索的依据。[已接收的终端化简](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/fact_graph/facts/1252b8823bb5a290.md)

**三、最清楚的主目标缺口**

目前重点考察一个轻微偏离 Silver 参数的步长族。对每个 enclosing integer $m\ge1536$，定义

$$
\varepsilon_m=\frac1{4m},\quad \beta=1-\varepsilon_m,\quad
c=(1+\sqrt2)(1+\varepsilon_m),\quad a=\frac\beta c,
$$

$$
H_0^{(m)}=(),\qquad
H_{j+1}^{(m)}=(H_j^{(m)},1+ac^j,H_j^{(m)}),\quad 0\le j<m.
$$

记 $S_j=\sum H_j^{(m)}$、$n_m=2^m-1$、$D_m=1+2S_m$。每个子块始终使用同一个 enclosing $m$，不能按子块深度重置 $\varepsilon_m$。

已接收的算术比较给出

$$
\frac{B_{n_m}}{D_m}<\frac{47}{50}.
$$

因此，只要最终能够对全部原始函数类、对所有充分大的 $m$，证明

$$
\boxed{\mathcal W_{n_m}(H_m^{(m)})\le\frac{17}{16D_m},}
$$

就会得到

$$
\liminf_{n\to\infty}\frac{\mathcal W_n^\star}{U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}}
\le\frac{799}{800}<1,
$$

从而否定原始渐近匹配命题。这里的全函数类上界**尚未证明**。若取得 $(1+C/m)/D_m$ 的统一上界，其中 $C$ 为绝对常数，也足够形成反例。最终阈值可以是更大的绝对常数，但不能依赖具体函数或起点。[已接收的基准比较和条件桥梁](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/fact_graph/facts/931b9653ce0f7201.md)

现有七步成果准确地是：对每个同样的 $m\ge1536$，

$$
\mathcal W_7(H_3^{(m)})=\frac1{1+2S_3}.
$$

更强地，对任意紧凸体 $D\ni0$、任意维数、起点和 $d\ge1$，定义

$$
U_d(x)=\max_{g\in D}(2\langle x,g\rangle-d\|g\|^2),
$$

则实际七步复合满足

$$
U_d(T_{H_3^{(m)}}x)\le U_{d+2S_3}(x).
$$

证明把两个实际子块、桥接点及辅助投影的约束合并，并逐项支付余项；Huber 实例给出下界等号。当前还缺能覆盖所有层数的定量组合机制。原目标也不要求先证明最强的 all-$d$ 传播：任何直接支付真实初始距离的完整上界都可以。

**四、近期候选推导的进展**

这些结果均未被正式接收；它们用于定位下一步，而不是作为新定理直接引用。

| 路线 | 当前候选结果 | 仍缺什么 |
|---|---|---|
| 奇对称三段函数 | 完整草稿给出 $(1+21/(4m))/D_m$，并经过一次独立分析审阅 | 仍是指定标量函数族，尚非全函数类上界 |
| 两侧不同截断高度 | 当较小与较大截断之比至少为 $c-2$ 时，已有完整 all-start 草稿，同样给出该上界 | 比值更小时的全部轨迹未覆盖 |
| 较小截断侧的尾部终点 | 草稿覆盖所有此类终点：上界 $1/D_m$；有穿越零点的轨迹实际上只能穿越一次，且严格小于 $2/(3D_m)$ | 其他终点仍需原始半径估计 |
| 一般双截断函数的梯度 | 最新报告覆盖至多两次穿越：$p_{\rm end}<R/(2d_j)$，并对多次穿越给出截断相关界 | 至少三次穿越、起点半径较小、终点位于截断前的情形仍未解决 |

这里的标量函数族固定为

$$
g(\sigma r)=\sigma\min\{B_\sigma,\max(\kappa r,r-(1-\kappa))\},
\quad 0<\kappa\le1,\quad B_\sigma\ge\kappa,
$$

并取 $f(x)=\int_0^x g(t)\,dt$。同一函数用于全部步骤；$R=|x_0|$，$d_j=ac^j$，$p_{\rm end}=|g(x_{\rm end})|$。

其中一个有用的新结构是“最后一次穿越”的支付：若完整子块最终落在较大截断侧，则最后一次穿越来自较小截断侧。设其层级为 $k$，完整后缀步长和为 $T$，便有

$$
p_{\rm end}\le\frac{d_kB_{\min}}{1+T}
\le\frac{d_kB_{\min}}{1+S_k}<(c-2)B_{\min}.
$$

较小截断侧尾部的结果进一步用实际前后缀步长乘积排除了两次以上穿越，再把唯一穿越的代价付回原始 $R$。这比只检查根部的一次特定跳转更广，但尚未补齐整个双截断族。[完整尾部候选](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/shared_evidence/b726f6dc7baa6b7b.md)；[最新梯度部分结果](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/shared_evidence/21e479d3aa05eed3.md)

系数证书方面，已得到若干**指定形式的排除推导**：源端三项约束、移动下标的源端流量、远端根部的前缀约束，以及最后一行的时钟矩约束。这些约束排除了具体的共同列和 PSD 余项选择，包括全部兼容的 skew 补全。投影 $H_4$ 路线中，若干起点约束和拼接权重的分配也被完整二次型中的负方向排除。它们没有排除一般证书锥，也没有给出真正违反目标不等式的合法函数。[一般终端接口及两种列的障碍](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/shared_evidence/8bcf300ff83e2288.md)

**五、Gray 与有限域路线的重要更正**

这一方向用 binary Gray code 和 Walsh characters 构造高维顶点，再要求这些顶点成为实际梯度。已经形成一个有用的候选接口：对固定、线性无关的顶点 $v_i$，把全部合法初始化转写为势函数 $F_i=\langle z_0,v_i\rangle$ 的全对差分约束，并保留到完整极锥最小点集合的真实距离。其 perspective 表达带有一个总是可行的零点；若原轨迹不可实现，必须明确这个空集边界，不能从优化表达中推断实际轨迹存在。

但是旧的径向衰减版本包含一个明确的计数错误。若前两级径向比记为 $x,y$，实际递推应为

$$
\sigma_k=x^{\lceil k/2\rceil}y^{\lfloor(k+2)/4\rfloor}.
$$

旧稿误用了 $\lfloor(k+1)/4\rfloor$，遗漏了相应的 level-one step。例如 $k=4q+2$ 的系数应为 $xy\theta^q$，不是 $x\theta^q$。该错误还进入了旧稿的有限生成多项式。

因此，旧稿所称覆盖全部合法初始化的 $343/(333D_m)$ 上界，现在应标为**待修复、待重新审阅的候选**。不能继续沿用“这一固定路线已经可靠排除”的表述。新报告明确没有重新核查旧上界；本次暂停整理也没有修复证明或重跑相关程序。错误公式本身已被指出，但尚不能据此断言旧上界的最终结论一定为假。

改为另一径向衰减参数后，最新草稿给出两个较窄结论：一个显式势函数在原始远距离约束处失败；同一两顶点 relaxation 则允许高于 $69/(64D_m)>17/(16D_m)$ 的收益。这只说明那一对子问题无法排除新的构造，**没有构造出满足全部原始约束的真实高维轨迹**。[更正与新径向版本报告](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/shared_evidence/a37b81ea81b53910.md)

**六、数值工作实际说明了什么**

最近的计算都有单独预算和校准。以下仅列会影响下一步判断的结果。

在同一 enclosing $m=1536$ 的有限 $H_3,H_4$ 上，以 $q=17/[16(1+2\sum h)]$ 搜索完整原始 dual，两种模式——自由终端参数 $\pi$ 和固定 $\pi=\sqrt q$——都返回了数值解。因而没有观察到这两类的一般可行性分离。H4 自由模式返回的 $\pi\approx-7.02\times10^{-12}$ 及其负乘子原样保留，没有被改写成精确零；其他模式也保留了小负特征值。这些不是精确证书。[有限 dual 诊断](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/terminal_mass_interior_discovery_20261003/HANDOFF.md)

最新一批直接最大化投影端点不等式的违反量

$$
V=U_1(T_hx_0)-U_{1+2\sum h}(x_0),\qquad \|x_0\|=1,
$$

纳入全部原始投影法向约束，得到：

| 有限算例 | 返回的 $V$ | 求解状态 |
|---|---:|---|
| $H_3$ | $1.0874\times10^{-9}$ | optimal |
| $H_4$ | $2.4904\times10^{-9}$ | optimal_inaccurate |

两项已知校准均通过，但上述生产值都接近零。H4 的最小原始 Gram 特征值为 $-1.5756\times10^{-10}$，最大原始法向残差为 $3.7285\times10^{-10}$，dual stationarity 缺陷约为 $1.8965\times10^{-8}$。因此这批结果没有给出可信的精确反例，也没有证明 $H_4$ 不等式。这两个生产算例的原始 Gram 都含小负特征值，坐标重建被跳过，没有通过裁剪特征值制造“实际轨迹”。[H3 原始摘要](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/projection_u_primal_discovery_20261003/03_H3/summary.json)；[H4 原始摘要](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/projection_u_primal_discovery_20261003/04_H4/summary.json)

此前 Gray 的几个有限字典算例只产生了带正残差的数值势函数；优化公共平移后也没有提供所需的真实下界见证。有限字典随维数改变，不能外推为完整 $2^m$ 维构造的合法性或渐近结论。

**七、恢复研究时值得先做的调整**

我的判断是，最值得保留的主线是完整投影模型及其全原始约束；继续仅在已经被排除的低秩证书形式上改几个系数，收益已经降低。下一步应先做来源修复和方向选择，再决定是否恢复调用。

1. 先处理 Gray 计数错误：定位受影响的公式、证明和数值构造，重新确定旧上界还能保留多少。不要把旧候选的结论直接带入新任务。
2. 若主攻反例方向，围绕真正需要的 $17/(16D_m)$ 全函数类上界重新选择证书结构。all-$d$ 的精确传播是可选工具，不是原目标的必需前置结论。
3. 若主攻下界构造，新的高维方案必须先满足全部原始插值或投影约束，再核对真实最近最小点距离。两顶点 relaxation 和数值势函数只能提供线索。
4. 双截断标量族目前剩下的具体类是至少三次穿越、较小原始半径、截断前终点。它可以作为独立子问题继续，但应明确它与全维主目标之间还缺桥梁。

**八、暂停状态与接续入口**

这项工作确实使用了 Danus：由其管理项目、共享结果、研究轮次和核查接收记录；研究后端使用 Codex，并辅以少量有明确额度的直接 Codex 调用。最新模型选择是 gpt-6-astra / ultra。

Goal 已标记为 PAUSED。四个 Danus 研究员均已无存活进程，最后一个未完成调用被终止；直接数值诊断已关闭，文档代理也已中断。已完成原始输出仍在，本次未启动新研究或正式核查。恢复须等你明确提出；不会因为存在旧任务文件或未用完的额度而自动重启。

最有用的接续入口是本总结、[原始完整问题](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/PROBLEM.md) 和 [暂停接续索引](D:/Danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/PAUSED_HANDOFF_20261003.md)。索引另行保存精确文件、证据状态、未读报告和被中止的任务，便于下一次接续，而不把操作记录混入上述数学结论。
